VisionBar — timing explained

How to read this report
  • All durations are milliseconds (1,000 ms = 1 second).
  • The same stage repeats for each trigger, camera or dashboard message; it is not automatically a duplicate.
  • Totals include child measurements. Never add inference to image_read_decode, frame_audit to S3/database timings, or trigger_to_audit to stage totals.
  • Waits are elapsed queue delays, not CPU/GPU processing time. audit_handoff_wait overlaps audit_queue_put.
  • Error/cancelled rows are separate from successful rows. An ok row means that measured block returned, not that every job was saved.
  • UTC timestamps mark completion of each measurement; nested children can appear before their parent total.
  • Files are kept separate so different runs are not accidentally combined. A missing measurement means unknown, not zero.
Inference image-read time is unavailable in these older logs. frame_bundle only selects references. frame_read measures the audit process reading bytes for S3. Run the updated application to capture image_read_decode separately.
Stage guide — what each measurement means
CSV stageOperation / code ownerKindIncluded inMeaning
audit_handoff_waitWait between vision and audit / AuditWorker.processwait—Per trigger; from vision finishing to audit starting. Includes queue insertion and queue residence.
audit_queue_putSend vision result to audit queue / VisionWorker.runhandoff—Once per trigger. Includes waiting for queue space, not audit processing.
audit_totalComplete audit work for one trigger / AuditWorker.processtotal—All cameras, uploads, raw writes, merging and final writes. Excludes the preceding audit wait.
coordinate_mappingMap detections to physical coordinates / CoordinateMapper.map_detectionoperationvision_totalOne measurement per camera, covering all detections from that frame.
database_connectOpen database pool / AuditWorker.runlifecycle—Once at audit startup.
deduplication_gradingMerge detections and grade settled defects / DeduplicationEngine.process_triggeroperationaudit_totalOnce per audited trigger, across all cameras.
final_defect_writeSave final defects and member links / AuditWriter.write_final_defectsoperationaudit_totalPer trigger only when groups settle. Includes all settled groups for that trigger.
frame_auditUpload and save one camera frame / AuditWriter.write_frametotalaudit_totalPer camera with detections. Includes s3_upload and raw_database_transaction; do not add those again.
frame_bundleSelect frame references and create bundle / FrameIngestManager.create_bundleoperationvision_totalOnce per trigger for all cameras. Selects paths and assigns metadata; no image bytes are read.
frame_readRead original file bytes for S3 / AuditWorker.read_frameoperationaudit_totalPer camera with detections; reads encoded bytes again for upload, with file checks and thread scheduling. This is NOT the inference image read.
inferenceComplete detection for one camera / SahiDetectionEngine.detecttotalvision_totalOnce per camera per trigger. Includes read/decode, preprocessing, tiling, prediction, SAHI merging and thread scheduling; not GPU-only time.
live_event_handoffSend live defects to control process / AuditWorker.processhandoffaudit_totalOne timing for all live events of an audited camera frame; not browser delivery.
model_startupLoad model and warm up / SahiDetectionEngine.startlifecycle—Once at vision startup; not repeated for each frame.
operator_commandSend MCU command and await response / AppOrchestrator.handle_operator_commandoperation—Once per measured operator command; not roll preparation.
panel_queue_waitWait before dashboard broadcast / AppOrchestrator._send_panelswait—Per dashboard message, including status updates and separate defect events.
raw_database_transactionSave trigger, media and raw detections / AuditWriter.write_frameoperationframe_auditPer audited camera frame; includes database transaction and commit.
roll_preparationCreate or prepare roll / AuditWriter.start_rolloperation—Per roll preparation request; includes persistence.
s3_uploadUpload whole frame to S3 / AuditWriter._upload_frameoperationframe_auditPer audited camera frame; includes network and any SDK retries.
schema_checkCheck database schema / SchemaChecker.checklifecycle—Once at startup; includes acquiring a connection.
trigger_enqueueSubmit trigger to vision / AppOrchestrator.on_triggeroperation—Once per submission attempt. An error row is not an accepted trigger.
trigger_to_auditAdmission to end of audit / AuditWorker.runend-to-end—Includes vision, queue waiting and audit. Do not add stage totals to this value.
vision_queue_waitWait before vision begins / VisionWorker.processwait—Per trigger; from admission until vision begins processing.
vision_totalComplete vision work for one trigger / VisionWorker.processtotal—Includes frame bundle, both/all cameras and overhead; excludes earlier queue wait and later audit-queue insertion.
websocket_broadcastBroadcast dashboard message / AppOrchestrator._send_panelsoperation—Per dashboard message; not confirmation that the browser displayed it.
workers_shutdownShut down workers / AppOrchestrator.stoplifecycle—Once at shutdown. An ok timing row does not prove every job was saved.
workers_startupStart vision and audit workers / AppOrchestrator.startlifecycle—Once at startup; includes waiting for both workers to become ready.
